Human acceptance: **not recorded**. Review was deferred to this inventory by explicit instruction. No questions were asked in chat, and no audio page or clips were created.

Both complete line-by-line passes are finished. Most travel, introductions, trade planning, concealment, canal transit, and arrival narration remain intelligible. Remaining marked spans can affect named entities, historical ownership, a proposed action, an inventory, journey duration, or exact Sending wording. These require morning human review and have not been accepted. Incidental uncertainty is inventoried separately using explicitly labeled prepared evidence and cleaned wording; a quoted cleaned unit can include supported corrections. The user directed logging instead of in-chat questions; no audio page or clips were requested.

Material spans remain bracketed in the cleaned transcript. Incidental inventory spans identify unclear words or whole clipped fragments in the cleaned wording; they are not additional bracket markers. Each entry separately quotes exact prepared evidence and current cleaned wording, with context from the same respective transcript. A cleaned quotation is not a claim that its entire unit is unchanged from the prepared source.

Secondary spelling confirmations: Darba, Chardon, Finellen Silverstone, Lyle Honeyhill, Pearl Copperharp, Kaeso, Mitus Verina Auratan, Seva Auratan, Chalyte, Harol Silversong, Emerald Song, Vondal Redpeak, Dani Silversong, Ewen Silversong, Vola, Bhishma, Garret Tealeaf, Old Chardon Canal, Casian, Chalice of the Runepriest, Oswalt Tealeaf, Jasmine Sunmeadow, Laka, Travok Redpeak, Hralgar, Myraeni Gap, Bas Udda.

The prepared/raw sources, session YAML, and speaker statistics were fingerprinted before cleanup and are checked again at handoff. No glossary was supplied; no glossary was edited. No pipeline, recap, campaign-note, shared-index, commit, or push work was performed.
